* Start of Frame
	String Parser: Add delimiter "&"
	Ini : Set current file to "savegame"

* User clicks with left button on Group.Bombs
	Group.Bombs : Change animation sequence to Stand up

* Android object : On back button pressed
	storyboard controls : Jump to frame "Cloud saves" (64)
	back : Change animation sequence to Stand up

* User clicks with left button on back
	storyboard controls : Jump to frame "Cloud saves" (64)

* User clicks with left button on Next page
	storyboard controls : Jump to frame "Restore from cloud 2" (68)

* AndroidPlus: On App return from pause?
	Special : Set game resumed to 1

* User clicks with left button on restore to cloud 1
+ GPG Connector: Is local player authenticated?
	 GPG Cloud : Load Snapshot with name "save1" and message "Loading 1st save from cloud..."
	restore saves : Set Counter to 1

* GPG Cloud: On Snapshot loaded
+ restore saves = 1
	String Parser: Set source string to SnapshotData$( "GPG Cloud" )
	Special : Set selected save slot to "save1"
